Loss → the most TCP can do		丢包 → TCP 的速度上限

A letter in envelopes inside envelopes - each layer adds one, the other side opens them in reverse.		像一封信套着多层信封，每层加一个，对端再按相反顺序拆开。
Each layer adds an envelope on the way out; the other side opens them in reverse.		发送时每层套一层信封，对端按相反顺序拆开。
here's my message		这是我的消息

Opens the envelopes in reverse order		按相反顺序拆开信封
Adds more envelopes		再套更多信封

Ports - which app gets the packet		端口：数据包交给哪个应用

Bandwidth, delay, jitter and loss - four different things that all feel like "slow".		带宽、延迟、抖动和丢包，四种不同的问题都会让人觉得“慢”。
Bandwidth is how much; delay is how long; jitter is how uneven; loss is how much goes missing.		带宽是多少数据，延迟是等待多久，抖动是时间多不稳定，丢包是丢了多少。
ping (3 ms)		ping（3 ms）
Speed tests show bandwidth, but calls and browsing suffer from delay, jitter and loss.		测速显示带宽，但通话和浏览还会受延迟、抖动与丢包影响。
Which can feel 'slow' even on a fast plan?		即使套餐很快，什么仍会让人感觉慢？
High delay, jitter or loss		高延迟、抖动或丢包
A big hard disk		大容量硬盘
A bright screen		明亮的屏幕
A long password		很长的密码
Streaming vs calls - why one buffers and the other stutters		视频与通话：为什么一个缓冲，另一个卡顿
Netflix can wait; a conversation can't.		Netflix 可以等，对话不能。
Video downloads ahead and buffers; calls play every packet the moment it arrives.		视频能提前下载并缓冲，通话则需要及时播放到达的数据包。
Video server		视频服务器
10 s of video ahead (TCP)		提前下载 10 s 视频（TCP）
YouTube fine but Teams breaking up is normal on a jittery network.		网络抖动时，YouTube 正常而 Teams 断音并不奇怪。
Why does Netflix buffer instead of stuttering like a call?		Netflix 为什么能缓冲，而不像通话那样断断续续？
It can download ahead and wait; a conversation can't		它能提前下载并等待，对话不能
It uses better cables		它使用更好的网线
It doesn't use the internet		它不使用互联网
Calls use no buffer at all		通话完全不使用缓冲

ARP - finding the hardware next door		ARP：查找身边设备的硬件地址
IP addresses for the journey, hardware addresses for the next step.		IP 地址用于全程寻址，硬件地址用于下一步转发。
Finds the hardware address of the router next to you, so packets can leave the room.		查找附近路由器的硬件地址，让数据包能离开本地网络。

If the router's hardware address suddenly changes, someone may be in the middle.		路由器硬件地址突然变化，可能有人在中间拦截流量。

DHCP - how your PC gets an address		DHCP：电脑如何获得地址
Four messages, and you're on the network.		四条消息，让你接入网络。
Gives your PC an address, the router's address and DNS servers when you join a network.		加入网络时，为电脑提供自身地址、路由器地址和 DNS 服务器。
DISCOVER - any DHCP server here?		DISCOVER：这里有 DHCP 服务器吗？
REQUEST - I'll take .37		REQUEST：我选择 .37
ACK - it's yours for 24 hours		ACK：该地址归你使用 24 小时
A 169.254.x.x address means DHCP failed - restart Wi-Fi or the router.		169.254.x.x 地址说明 DHCP 失败，可尝试重连 Wi-Fi 或重启路由器。
What does DHCP give your PC when it joins a network?		电脑加入网络时，DHCP 提供什么？
An IP address, the router and DNS servers		IP 地址、路由器和 DNS 服务器
A Wi-Fi password		Wi-Fi 密码
A faster internet plan		更快的宽带套餐
The website's certificate		网站证书
TCP - the reliable conversation		TCP：可靠通信
Numbered, acknowledged, resent when lost.		编号、确认，丢失后重传。
A reliable conversation: numbered pieces, every one confirmed, lost ones sent again.		可靠的通信：数据分段编号，每段都确认，丢失的重新发送。
Server		服务器
SYN - let's talk		SYN：开始通信吧
SYN-ACK - sure		SYN-ACK：好的
ACK - connected		ACK：连接建立
ACK - got it		ACK：收到了
Lots of resends (retransmits) mean loss on the way - usually Wi-Fi.		重传很多意味着途中有丢包，常见于 Wi-Fi。
What does TCP do when a packet goes missing?		数据包丢失时，TCP 怎么办？
Sends it again, so nothing arrives missing		重新发送，确保数据完整到达
Skips it to stay fast		跳过它，保持速度
Closes the connection		关闭连接
Asks DNS again		再次查询 DNS
TLS - the padlock		TLS：安全锁
Proves who the server is, then encrypts everything.		先验证服务器身份，再加密后续通信。
The padlock: proves the server is real and encrypts everything after.		安全锁：验证服务器真实身份，然后加密后续内容。
ClientHello - I speak TLS 1.3		ClientHello：我支持 TLS 1.3
ServerHello + certificate		ServerHello + 证书
Finished - keys agreed		Finished：密钥协商完成
encrypted request		加密请求
encrypted answer		加密应答
A certificate error means the wrong server, a wrong clock, or something in the middle.		证书错误可能表示服务器不对、系统时间不对，或途中存在拦截。
What does the padlock (TLS) prove before encrypting?		加密前，安全锁（TLS）证明什么？
That the server really is who it says it is		服务器确实是它声称的身份
That your Wi-Fi is fast		你的 Wi-Fi 很快
That the site has no ads		网站没有广告
That your PC has no viruses		电脑没有病毒
UDP - fast and fire-and-forget		UDP：快速发送，不等确认
No handshake, no resending - speed first.		无需握手，不负责重传，速度优先。
Fire and forget: no confirmations, no resends - fast, for calls, games and DNS.		发出即不再等待：没有确认和重传，速度快，适合通话、游戏和 DNS。
packet 1		数据包 1
packet 2		数据包 2
packet 3 (no replies needed)		数据包 3（无需应答）
A lost UDP packet is just gone - in a call that's a glitch or robot voice.		丢失的 UDP 包不会自动补发，通话中可能表现为断音或机械音。
Why do games and calls usually use UDP?		游戏和通话为什么通常使用 UDP？
Late data is useless, so speed beats resending		迟到的数据已无用，因此及时性比重传更重要
UDP is encrypted and TCP isn't		UDP 已加密，TCP 没有
UDP packets can't be lost		UDP 数据包不会丢失
Routers block TCP		路由器阻止 TCP
HTTP - asking for a page		HTTP：请求网页
A request, a status, an answer.		请求、状态码、应答。
How apps ask for things: GET this page, POST this form - the server answers with a code.		应用请求资源的方式：GET 获取网页，POST 提交表单，服务器用状态码应答。
200 OK + the page		200 OK + 网页
Codes: 200 fine, 401/403 not allowed, 404 not found, 5xx the server is broken.		状态码：200 正常，401/403 无权限，404 未找到，5xx 服务器故障。
What does a 404 answer from a web server mean?		网页服务器返回 404 是什么意思？
The page you asked for isn't there		请求的页面不存在
The server is overloaded		服务器过载
Your password is wrong		你的密码错误
The connection is encrypted		连接已加密
Are you there? How far? Which way?		你在线吗？有多远？走哪条路？
The network's own messages: ping asks 'are you there?', traceroute asks every router on the way.		网络自身的消息：ping 问“你在线吗？”，路由追踪则询问沿途各路由器。
echo request (TTL 1)		回显请求（TTL 1）
time exceeded - I'm hop 1		超时，我是第 1 跳
No ping reply doesn't always mean down - many servers ignore ping on purpose.		没有 ping 应答不一定是离线，很多服务器会主动忽略 ping。
What does traceroute show?		路由追踪显示什么？
The routers that answer along the path your probes took (silent hops show as *)		探测路径上作出应答的路由器，不响应的跳显示为 *
The speed of your Wi-Fi		你的 Wi-Fi 速度
Which apps use the internet		哪些应用在联网
Many addresses at once, and some of them change every day.		同时拥有多个地址，其中部分每天变化。
The newer, much bigger address system; your PC usually has both IPv4 and IPv6.		更新、更大的地址系统，电脑通常同时拥有 IPv4 和 IPv6。
itself		自身
here's the network prefix		这是网络前缀
Broken IPv6 makes some sites slow to start while the PC falls back to IPv4.		IPv6 故障可能让部分网站启动慢，因为电脑需要等待回退到 IPv4。
Why can a PC have several IPv6 addresses at once?		电脑为什么能同时有多个 IPv6 地址？
Some are temporary (privacy) addresses that change over time		部分是随时间变化的临时隐私地址
IPv6 is broken		IPv6 出故障了
Each app gets its own		每个应用分配一个
One per Wi-Fi channel		每个 Wi-Fi 信道分配一个
MTU - how big a packet can be		MTU：数据包能有多大
1500 bytes, minus whatever tunnels add.		1500 字节，减去隧道所需的额外空间。
The biggest packet that fits on a link - usually 1500 bytes, less on VPNs and PPPoE.		链路能容纳的最大包大小，通常为 1500 字节，VPN 和 PPPoE 下会更小。
1500 bytes		1500 字节
resends smaller		用更小的包重传
Pages that half-load or hang on a VPN are often an MTU problem.		VPN 中网页加载不完整或卡住，常常与 MTU 有关。
What usually makes the usable MTU smaller than 1500 bytes?		什么通常会让可用 MTU 小于 1500 字节？
A tunnel such as a VPN or PPPoE adding its own headers		VPN 或 PPPoE 等隧道增加了自身报头
A slow Wi-Fi signal		Wi-Fi 信号慢
Too many browser tabs		浏览器标签页太多
A weak password		密码太弱
NAT - many devices, one public address		NAT：多个设备，一个公网地址
Why your PC's address isn't the one the internet sees.		为什么电脑自身的地址不同于互联网看到的地址。
Your router swaps your private address for its one public address on the way out.		发往外网时，路由器将你的私网地址换成自己的公网地址。
Your PC (192.168.1.37)		你的电脑（192.168.1.37）
to github.com		发往 github.com
A 100.64.x.x hop means your provider adds a second NAT - some games and calls struggle.		出现 100.64.x.x 这一跳，说明运营商又加了一层 NAT，部分游戏和通话可能受影响。
Why does a website see a different address than your PC's own?		网站为什么看到的地址与电脑自身不同？
Your router swaps your private address for its public one (NAT)		路由器将私网地址换成自己的公网地址（NAT）
DNS changes it		DNS 更改了它
Your PC lies about it		电脑隐瞒了它
TLS hides it		TLS 隐藏了它
QUIC and HTTP/3 - the modern web		QUIC 与 HTTP/3：现代网页通信
TCP and TLS rebuilt on top of UDP.		基于 UDP 重新构建的可靠传输与 TLS 加密。
The modern web: connection and encryption in one quick handshake, over UDP 443.		现代网页通信：通过 UDP 443，用一次快速握手完成连接与加密。
hello + keys (UDP 443)		握手问候 + 密钥（UDP 443）
hello + certificate		握手问候 + 证书
request, already encrypted		请求，已经加密
If UDP is blocked, browsers quietly fall back to TCP - slower, but it works.		若 UDP 被阻止，浏览器会自动回退到 TCP，虽然慢些，但仍能工作。
What is QUIC built on?		QUIC 基于什么？
UDP, with TCP-like reliability and TLS built in		UDP，并内置类似 TCP 的可靠传输和 TLS
A new kind of cable		一种新型网线
Wi-Fi - how your laptop joins and stays connected		Wi-Fi：笔记本如何加入并保持连接
Scan, authenticate, associate, then share the air.		扫描、认证、关联，然后共享无线信道。
Radio between your PC and the access point - band, channel and signal decide how good it is.		电脑与接入点之间的无线通信，频段、信道和信号决定连接质量。
Access point		接入点
radio frame		无线帧
got it (ACK)		收到了（ACK）
Weak signal or a crowded channel: move closer, or use 5 GHz.		信号弱或信道拥挤时，靠近路由器或使用 5 GHz。
What does a Wi-Fi device do before it can send data?		Wi-Fi 设备发送数据之前需要做什么？
Scans, authenticates and associates with an access point		扫描、认证并与接入点关联
Asks DNS for permission		向 DNS 请求许可
Opens a TCP connection to the router		与路由器建立 TCP 连接
Runs a speed test		运行测速
802.1X - company sign-in for Wi-Fi and network ports		802.1X：公司 Wi-Fi 与网络端口的身份认证
The network checks who you are before letting you in.		网络先验证身份，再允许接入。
Work networks check who you are (certificate or password) before letting you on.		办公网络先通过证书或密码验证身份，再允许接入。
Switch / AP		交换机 / AP
EAPOL start		EAPOL 开始
you're in		允许接入
Can't join the work Wi-Fi but others can: your certificate or account, not the radio.		别人能连办公 Wi-Fi 而你不能，问题可能是证书或账号，不是无线信号。
What does 802.1X check before letting a device onto the network?		允许设备联网前，802.1X 检查什么？
Who the user or device is, through a server such as RADIUS		通过 RADIUS 等服务器验证用户或设备身份
The device's speed		设备速度
Its DNS cache		它的 DNS 缓存
Its screen size		它的屏幕尺寸
BGP - how the internet's networks agree on the way		BGP：互联网各网络如何协商路径
Thousands of networks, each telling its neighbours what it can reach.		成千上万个网络，相互告知自己能到达哪里。
The internet is about 80,000 networks (autonomous systems, each with an AS number). BGP is how they tell each other "I can reach these addresses, through me".		互联网由约 80,000 个网络组成，它们称为自治系统，各有 AS 号。BGP 让它们相互通告：“这些地址可通过我到达。”
Your provider		你的运营商
Transit network		转接网络
UPDATE - 198.51.100.0/24 is reachable through AS64500		UPDATE：198.51.100.0/24 可经 AS64500 到达
forwarded, following the path it learned		按照学到的路径转发
A wrong announcement (a "route leak" or hijack) can send traffic the wrong way for the whole internet in minutes. A slow site that's slow for everyone on your provider is often a peering choice, not your line.		错误通告，包括路由泄漏或劫持，可能几分钟内就让大量互联网流量走错路。同一运营商用户访问某网站都慢时，常与对等互联选择有关，而非你的线路。
Each provider runs BGP on its border routers. A route carries the path of AS numbers it went through; routers prefer the shortest AS path unless a business rule says otherwise (a cheaper neighbour, a paying customer first). Inside a provider, the same routes are spread with internal BGP (iBGP, often via route reflectors). Peering at an internet exchange (IXP) is two networks connecting directly for free; transit is paying a bigger network to reach everyone else. That's why one site is fast and another, just as close, is slow: the way there is a business decision. AdeeboMeter's "Who carries my traffic (BGP)" shows the networks your packets cross.		各运营商在边界路由器上运行 BGP。路由携带经过的 AS 号路径，通常优先选较短的 AS 路径，但业务策略可改变选择，例如优先便宜的邻居或付费客户。运营商内部用 iBGP 传播这些路由，常借助路由反射器。互联网交换点 IXP 的对等互联让两个网络免费直连；转接服务则是付费通过更大的网络访问其他网络。因此两个距离相近的网站可能速度不同，路径选择也受商业决策影响。AdeeboMeter 的“谁在传输我的流量（BGP）”可显示数据包跨越的网络。
What does BGP decide?		BGP 决定什么？
Which network-to-network path traffic takes across the internet		流量跨越互联网各网络时采用的路径
Which Wi-Fi channel your router uses		路由器使用的 Wi-Fi 信道
Which IP address your PC gets		电脑获取的 IP 地址
How fast a single cable runs		单根网线的速率
QoS and DSCP - priority for calls		QoS 与 DSCP：让通话优先
A label on each packet that says "me first".		在每个包上贴一张“我优先”的标签。
A priority label on packets - calls get 'express', downloads get normal.		数据包的优先级标签：通话走“快车道”，下载按普通优先级处理。
mark audio EF (express)		将音频标为 EF（优先）
Most home routers and the internet ignore it; it helps mainly inside company networks.		多数家用路由器和互联网会忽略它，主要在公司网络内部有用。
What is a DSCP mark?		DSCP 标记是什么？
A label on a packet asking routers to treat it with priority		数据包上的标签，请求路由器优先处理
A type of cable		一种网线
An encryption key		加密密钥
Path MTU - the biggest packet that fits		路径 MTU：能通过的最大数据包
Every link has a size limit; the smallest one on the way wins.		每段链路都有大小上限，沿途最小值决定全程上限。
Ethernet carries 1500 bytes; PPPoE, VPNs and tunnels wrap packets and leave less room.		以太网可承载 1500 字节，PPPoE、VPN 和隧道增加封装，留给原始包的空间更小。
1500 bytes, don't fragment		1500 字节，禁止分片
Too-big packets are dropped - if no router says so, pages half-load and VPNs stall (a black hole).		过大的包会被丢弃；若路由器又不返回错误，网页会加载不全，VPN 会卡住，形成黑洞。
Which link decides the biggest packet that fits a whole path?		哪一段链路决定整条路径能通过的最大包？
The smallest MTU anywhere on the way		沿途最小的 MTU
The first link		第一段链路
The fastest link		最快的链路
The last link		最后一段链路
Your packets and the replies often take different roads.		你发出的包和返回的应答，常常走不同道路。
Each network sends traffic out by its own choice, so the reply route is decided by the far end.		各网络自行决定流量出口，因此返回路由由远端决定。
request		请求
reply		应答
Loss or delay only on the way back looks like a problem 'out there' that no trace from your PC shows.		只在返程出现的丢包或延迟，看起来像远处有问题，但从本机发起的路由追踪可能看不到。
Do replies always come back the same way your packets went?		应答总沿着请求的原路返回吗？
No - the way back is often a different road		不是，返回路径常常不同
Yes, always		是，总是如此
Only on Wi-Fi		仅 Wi-Fi 如此
Only for DNS		仅 DNS 如此
OSPF and IS-IS - the map inside one network		OSPF 与 IS-IS：单个网络内部的地图
Every router knows the whole map of its own network, and the shortest way across it.		每台路由器都掌握所属网络的完整地图，并计算最短路径。
Inside one provider, routers run a link-state protocol - OSPF or IS-IS - so each one holds the same map of all routers and links, with a cost on each link.		在单个运营商内部，路由器运行 OSPF 或 IS-IS 等链路状态协议，使每台都拥有相同的路由器与链路地图，每条链路带有代价。
Router A		路由器 A
Router B		路由器 B
Hello - I'm A, area 0		Hello：我是 A，属于区域 0
Hello - I see you		Hello：我看到你了
here is my whole link database		这是我的完整链路状态数据库
my link to C just went down (new LSA)		我到 C 的链路刚断了（新 LSA）
Two routers that disagree on timers, area or MTU never become neighbours, and part of the map goes missing. A flapping link makes every router recompute again and again - the network "churns".		两台路由器的定时器、区域或 MTU 不一致时，无法成为邻居，地图会缺一部分。链路反复上下线，会让全网不断重新计算，造成路由震荡。
Every router floods small notices about its own links (OSPF's LSAs, IS-IS's LSPs). Each then runs Dijkstra's shortest-path algorithm on the full map. When a fibre is cut, the routers at each end flood the change and every router recomputes - in well-tuned networks, well under a second, and fast-reroute backups are often ready before that. BGP decides which exit to use; OSPF or IS-IS find the way to that exit. Big providers often prefer IS-IS: it runs directly on the link layer, not over IP, and copes well with very large single areas. OSPF splits big networks into areas around a backbone area 0.		每台路由器泛洪通告自身链路状态，OSPF 使用 LSA，IS-IS 使用 LSP，再用 Dijkstra 算法在完整地图上计算最短路径。光纤中断时，两端通告变化，所有路由器重新计算。调优良好的网络可在一秒内完成，快速重路由备用路径通常更早就绪。BGP 决定出口，OSPF 或 IS-IS 找到通往出口的路。大型运营商常偏好 IS-IS，因为它直接运行于链路层，不依赖 IP，也适应大型单区域。OSPF 则围绕骨干区域 0 划分多个区域。
What do OSPF and IS-IS build inside a provider's network?		OSPF 和 IS-IS 在运营商内部建立什么？
A map of every router and link, to work out the shortest paths		所有路由器和链路的地图，用于计算最短路径
The list of websites customers visit		用户访问的网站列表
Wi-Fi passwords		Wi-Fi 密码
The public routes of other networks		其他网络的公网路由
MPLS - labels for fast lanes through the core		MPLS：核心网中的标签转发
A short label on the front of each packet decides where it goes next.		包前面的短标签决定下一步发往哪里。
At the edge of a provider's network the packet gets a label; core routers just swap labels along a pre-built path (an LSP) without reading the IP address underneath.		进入运营商网络时，数据包被加上标签。核心路由器沿预先建立的 LSP 路径交换标签，无需读取内部 IP 地址。
Customer router		客户路由器
Provider edge PE1		运营商边缘 PE1
IP packet to 10.2.0.9		发往 10.2.0.9 的 IP 包
Labels add 4 bytes each; if the core's MTU wasn't raised to carry them, big packets get dropped - an MTU black hole inside the provider.		每个标签增加 4 字节。如果核心网 MTU 未相应提高，大包就会被丢弃，形成运营商内部的 MTU 黑洞。
Labels are handed out by LDP or, more and more, carried in the IGP itself (segment routing). A packet can carry a stack: an outer label for the path across the core and an inner label that says which customer or service it belongs to. That inner label is what makes VPNs, Ethernet services and traffic engineering possible on one shared network. The last-but-one router usually pops the outer label (penultimate hop popping) so the exit router only sees the service label. Traceroute through MPLS often shows hops with labels (ICMP extensions) - or hides them entirely when the provider tunnels them.		标签可由 LDP 分发，也越来越多地由 IGP 承载，即分段路由。数据包可带多层标签：外层指定穿过核心网的路径，内层标识客户或服务，因此同一共享网络能承载 VPN、以太网服务和流量工程。倒数第二台路由器通常弹出外层标签，让出口只处理服务标签。路由追踪可能通过 ICMP 扩展显示 MPLS 标签；若运营商将探测报文封装穿过核心网，也可能完全隐藏内部跳点。
What does an MPLS router look at to forward a packet?		MPLS 路由器转发数据包时查看什么？
A short label added in front of the packet		加在数据包前面的短标签
The website's name		网站名称
The Wi-Fi signal		Wi-Fi 信号
The packet's payload		数据包载荷
L3VPN (VRF) - a private network inside the provider		L3VPN（VRF）：运营商内部的专用网络
Many companies on one provider network, each in its own walled routing table.		多个公司共用运营商网络，各有独立隔离的路由表。
An enterprise connects its offices through the provider. The provider edge keeps a separate routing table per customer (a VRF - virtual routing and forwarding), so overlapping private addresses never mix.		企业通过运营商连接各办公室。运营商边缘为每位客户保留独立的虚拟路由转发表 VRF，因此重叠的私网地址不会混在一起。
Branch router		分支路由器
OSPF - I have 10.20.0.0/16		OSPF：我有 10.20.0.0/16
A route-target typo means a branch silently can't see head office. Overlapping addresses are fine inside separate VRFs and break the moment two are joined.		RT 配置写错，分支就可能无法访问总部。地址重叠在独立 VRF 内没有问题，一旦将两个网络相连就可能冲突。
Customer routes are learned at each site (static, OSPF or BGP), then carried between provider edges by MP-BGP as VPN-IPv4 routes: the customer's prefix plus a route distinguisher to keep it unique, plus route targets that say which VRFs import it - that's how a hub-and-spoke or a shared-services VPN is built. In the core the traffic rides MPLS (or segment routing / SRv6) with an inner VPN label. Internet access for the same company is often just another VRF. EVPN is the same idea for Ethernet (layer 2) services and data-centre fabrics.		各站点通过静态路由、OSPF 或 BGP 学到客户路由，再通过 MP-BGP 以 VPN-IPv4 路由传播到其他边缘。路由包含客户前缀、用于区分重叠地址的 RD，以及决定哪些 VRF 导入路由的 RT，从而构建中心辐射或共享服务 VPN。核心网使用 MPLS、分段路由或 SRv6 承载流量，并携带内层 VPN 标签。同一公司的互联网接入通常只是另一个 VRF。EVPN 将类似理念用于以太网第 2 层服务和数据中心网络。
Two companies both use 10.0.0.0/8 over the same provider. What keeps them apart?		两个公司在同一运营商中都使用 10.0.0.0/8，如何相互隔离？
Each has its own routing table (a VRF) and its own VPN label		各有自己的路由表（VRF）和 VPN 标签
They take turns		它们轮流使用
The provider changes their addresses		运营商修改它们的地址
Wi-Fi encryption		Wi-Fi 加密
Multicast - one TV stream for thousands of homes		多播：一路电视流，服务千家万户
Sent once, copied only where someone is watching.		只发送一次，仅在有观众的分支复制。
A live channel is one multicast group address. Your set-top box joins the group; routers copy the stream only onto links where somebody is watching.		直播频道对应一个多播组地址。机顶盒加入组后，路由器仅向有观众的链路复制播放流。
Set-top box		机顶盒
Home router		家用路由器
IGMP join 239.1.1.5 (channel 5)		IGMP 加入 239.1.1.5（频道 5）
IGMP snooping off on a home switch floods the TV stream to every port - Wi-Fi collapses. A join that never reaches the tree gives a black screen while the internet works fine.		家用交换机关闭 IGMP snooping，会把电视流泛洪到每个端口，可能拖垮 Wi-Fi。加组请求未到达分发树时，可能出现电视黑屏但上网正常。
The box sends an IGMP "join" (MLD on IPv6) when you change channel; the access switch or router notes it (IGMP snooping keeps it off ports that didn't ask). Routers build a distribution tree toward the source with PIM - usually source-specific (PIM-SSM: "this channel from that server"). One 8 Mbps channel watched by 50,000 homes costs the core 8 Mbps, not 400 Gbps. Channel change speed depends on how fast the join reaches a router that already has the stream - providers add fast-channel-change servers that blast a short unicast burst while the multicast joins. Ordinary internet streaming (Netflix, YouTube) can't use multicast - every viewer pulls their own copy.		换台时，机顶盒发送 IGMP 加组消息，IPv6 使用 MLD。接入交换机或路由器记录成员关系，IGMP snooping 防止流量涌向未申请的端口。路由器用 PIM 建立通往源的分发树，常见为 PIM-SSM，即指定服务器的指定频道。一路 8 Mbps 频道即使有 50,000 户观看，核心仍只需 8 Mbps，而非 400 Gbps。换台速度取决于加组请求多快到达已有该流的路由器；快速换台服务器可先发短暂单播，等待多播就绪。普通 Netflix、YouTube 播放使用单播，每位观众单独获取一份。
Why does IPTV use multicast?		IPTV 为什么使用多播？
The stream is sent once and copied only where viewers are		播放流只发送一次，仅向有观众的方向复制
It encrypts the channel		它能加密频道
It makes the picture sharper		它让画面更清晰
It avoids needing a router		它可以不使用路由器
Netflix, HBO and YouTube - how streaming really reaches you		Netflix、HBO 与 YouTube：视频如何传到你这里
The film comes from a box inside your provider, a few chunks at a time.		影片来自运营商内部的一台缓存服务器，每次传输几个片段。
Big streamers put cache servers (a CDN - Netflix's is Open Connect) inside providers' networks. Your player downloads the film in short chunks over HTTPS, choosing the quality for each chunk from your speed right now.		大型平台会在运营商网络中部署 CDN 缓存，Netflix 的系统叫 Open Connect。播放器通过 HTTPS 分段下载影片，并根据当前速度为每段选择画质。
Your TV		你的电视
Streaming service		流媒体服务
play this film (sign-in, licence)		播放此影片（登录、授权）
use the cache cache-12 near you, here's the segment list		使用附近的 cache-12，这是片段列表
A busy evening Wi-Fi or a provider link to the CDN that's full shows up as quality drops first, buffering second. A VPN can send you to a far-away cache - slower and lower quality.		晚间 Wi-Fi 拥挤，或运营商到 CDN 的链路满载，通常先导致画质下降，再出现缓冲。VPN 可能将你引到更远的缓存，速度和画质都会下降。
The video is cut into 2-10 second segments, each encoded at several qualities (a "ladder" from 240p to 4K). The player (DASH or HLS) keeps a buffer of upcoming segments; if downloads slow, it picks a lower rung - that's why quality drops before the video stops. Buffering only happens when the buffer runs dry. Popular titles are pre-loaded onto the caches overnight; a DNS answer or a steering service sends you to the nearest healthy cache. HBO, Disney+ and YouTube work the same way with their own or rented CDNs. 4K needs about 15-25 Mbps steady - the average speed matters less than the dips.		视频切成 2-10 秒片段，每段编码为多档画质，从 240p 到 4K 形成码率阶梯。DASH 或 HLS 播放器预先缓存后续片段；下载慢时降低档位，所以通常先降画质，再发生停顿。只有缓冲耗尽才会卡住。热门影片会夜间预加载到缓存，DNS 或调度服务将你引向附近健康的节点。HBO、Disney+ 和 YouTube 也使用自建或租用 CDN。4K 通常需要持续 15-25 Mbps，短暂降速比平均速度更关键。
Where does a Netflix film usually come from?		Netflix 影片通常来自哪里？
A cache server inside or next to your provider's network		运营商网络内部或附近的缓存服务器
Netflix's head office		Netflix 总部
Your neighbour's PC		邻居的电脑
A satellite every time		每次都来自卫星
Live football online - why your neighbour cheers first		在线足球直播：邻居为什么先欢呼
Live streams are chunked, cached and buffered - 20 to 60 seconds behind the pitch.		直播需要分段、缓存和缓冲，通常落后球场 20 到 60 秒。
A live match goes camera → encoder → packager (chunks) → CDN → your player's buffer. Each step adds seconds; classic HLS sits 20-45 s behind broadcast.		比赛画面依次经过摄像机、编码器、分段封装器、CDN 和播放器缓冲。每步都增加几秒，传统 HLS 通常比广播落后 20-45 s。
Stadium cameras		球场摄像机
Encoder		编码器
live picture		实时画面
Shrinking the buffer for lower delay makes stalls more likely. A goal alert on your phone beats the stream because notifications don't wait in a video buffer.		为降低延迟而缩小缓冲，会提高卡顿风险。手机进球通知比视频更快，是因为通知不用等待视频缓冲。
Low-latency HLS and DASH cut chunks into small parts and push them as soon as they exist, getting to 3-8 seconds - but with a smaller buffer, a Wi-Fi hiccup stalls the picture sooner. A big match is the hardest day for a CDN: millions ask for the same seconds at the same moment, so providers pre-position capacity and some use multicast-assisted delivery inside their own network. Satellite and IPTV multicast are usually closer to live than online apps. Picture stutter on a big match night is often the shared links, not your line.		低延迟 HLS 和 DASH 将片段拆成更小部分，一生成就传输，可降到 3-8 秒。但缓冲更小，Wi-Fi 稍有波动就更容易卡住。大赛时数百万人同时请求相同片段，是 CDN 最繁忙的时刻。运营商会预置容量，部分在内部使用多播辅助分发。卫星和 IPTV 多播通常比网络应用更接近实时。大赛之夜卡顿常是共享链路拥堵，而非你的线路。
Why is a live football stream often 30 seconds behind TV?		在线足球直播为什么经常比电视晚 30 秒？
It is cut into chunks, encoded, sent through a CDN and buffered by the player		它需要分段、编码、经过 CDN，再由播放器缓冲
The camera is slow		摄像机慢
Your Wi-Fi is weak		你的 Wi-Fi 信号弱
The referee adds a delay		裁判增加了延迟
Checking...		检查中…
Separate small functions that call each other over HTTP APIs.		相互通过 HTTP API 调用的小型独立功能。
The AMF handles registration and mobility; the SMF sets up your data session; the UPF carries your packets; AUSF and UDM check who you are; the NRF is the directory where functions find each other.		AMF 处理注册和移动性，SMF 建立数据会话，UPF 转发数据包，AUSF 与 UDM 验证身份，NRF 提供功能发现目录。
registration request (N2)		注册请求（N2）
AMF trouble: phones can't register. SMF or UPF trouble: registered, no data. A failover that keeps registration but drops sessions looks exactly like "full bars, no internet" until the phone re-establishes its session.		AMF 故障会影响注册，SMF 或 UPF 故障则可能已注册却无法上网。切换保留注册却丢失会话时，就会出现“满格无网络”，直到手机重新建立会话。
The 5G core is "service-based": functions expose HTTP/2 APIs on a shared bus (N11 between AMF and SMF, N12 to the AUSF, N8 and N10 to the UDM). The gNodeB talks to the AMF over N2 (NGAP, with NAS inside) and sends data to the UPF over N3 (GTP-U); the UPF reaches the internet or a company network over N6. The SMF programs the UPF over N4 (PFCP). Because functions are software, they run in clouds with several copies; when one fails, another takes over - and a test of that failover asks one question: how long until data works again? The PCF sets policy; network slices let one core run separate virtual networks with different rules.		5G 核心网采用服务化架构，各功能提供 HTTP/2 API。AMF 与 SMF 通过 N11 通信，N12 连接 AUSF，N8、N10 连接 UDM。gNodeB 经 N2 使用 NGAP 承载 NAS 与 AMF 通信，经 N3 GTP-U 向 UPF 发数据；UPF 经 N6 到互联网或企业网络。SMF 经 N4 PFCP 配置 UPF。功能以软件形式多副本部署在云中，某个故障时由其他副本接管。故障切换测试关心的是数据多久恢复。PCF 管理策略，网络切片让同一核心网承载规则不同的虚拟网络。
In the 5G core, which function carries your actual data packets?		5G 核心网中，哪个功能承载实际数据包？
The UPF		UPF
The AMF		AMF
The AUSF		AUSF
The NRF		NRF
Who are you, prove it, here's your address.		你是谁，请证明，这是你的地址。
Joining has two halves: registration (who you are, proven with the SIM's key) and a data session (an IP address and a path). In 4G the default bearer comes with the attach; in 5G the PDU session is a separate step.		入网分两部分：注册，通过 SIM 密钥证明身份；数据会话，获取 IP 与传输路径。4G 附着时建立默认承载，5G 则单独建立 PDU 会话。
Phone		手机
Registration Request		Registration Request（注册请求）
Authentication Request (challenge)		Authentication Request（认证挑战）
Authentication Response		Authentication Response（认证响应）
Registration Accept		Registration Accept（注册接受）
PDU Session Establishment Request (DNN internet - passed on to the SMF)		PDU Session Establishment Request（DNN internet，转交 SMF）
PDU Session Establishment Accept from the SMF - IP 100.70.3.9		SMF 返回 PDU Session Establishment Accept，IP 为 100.70.3.9
"Registered" on the router's screen only means the first half worked. Look for the session accept and an IP address before blaming anything else.		路由器显示“已注册”，只证明前半部分成功。先找会话接受和 IP 地址，再排查其他原因。
In 4G: attach request → authentication (the network sends a challenge only your SIM can answer) → security mode → the MME asks the gateways for a session → attach accept with your IP → the default bearer is up. In 5G SA: registration request → authentication → security mode → registration accept; then the phone asks for a PDU session for a DNN (5G's name for the APN) → the SMF picks a UPF and an address → PDU session accept. Each step can be rejected with a cause code (for example "DNN not supported", "insufficient resources"). A modem log that ends after "PDU Session Establishment Request" with no accept or reject is a strong clue the problem is the session side, not the radio.		4G 流程为附着请求、认证（网络发出只有 SIM 能回答的挑战）、安全模式、MME 向网关请求会话、带 IP 的附着接受，然后默认承载就绪。5G SA 先注册请求、认证、安全模式和注册接受；手机再针对 DNN 请求 PDU 会话，DNN 是 5G 对 APN 的称呼。SMF 选择 UPF 和地址，随后接受会话。每步都可能返回拒绝原因，如不支持 DNN 或资源不足。日志停在 PDU Session Establishment Request 后，既无接受也无拒绝，强烈提示问题在会话侧，而非无线信号。
In 5G, after registration is accepted, what still has to happen before data flows?		5G 注册被接受后，数据传输前还需要什么？
A PDU session must be set up		建立 PDU 会话
The SIM must be removed		取出 SIM 卡
Wi-Fi must be turned on		开启 Wi-Fi
The phone must restart		重启手机
The name of the network you ask for, and the pipe you get.		你请求接入的网络名称，以及获得的数据通道。
The APN (4G) or DNN (5G) names the network you want: "internet", a company's private network, IMS for voice calls. The bearer (4G) or PDU session (5G) is the resulting pipe, with its own IP address and QoS.		APN（4G）或 DNN（5G）指定目标网络，例如 internet、企业专网或语音通话 IMS。承载（4G）或 PDU 会话（5G）则是建立的数据通道，拥有自己的 IP 地址和 QoS。
Core		核心网
connect me to APN "acme.corp"		请连接到 APN "acme.corp"
session up, IP 10.40.0.17, QoS 9		会话已建立，IP 10.40.0.17，QoS 9
Typo in the APN on a router: registered, no data. The wrong IP type (IPv4 vs IPv6 vs both) can also be refused.		路由器中的 APN 拼错，会出现已注册但无法上网。IP 类型选错，例如 IPv4、IPv6 或双栈，也可能被拒绝。
One device can hold several at once: internet data, IMS for VoLTE calls (with a guaranteed-bit-rate bearer for the voice itself), a private APN for a company's routers. Each gets its own address and can go to a different gateway. QoS is set per flow: a QCI (4G) or 5QI (5G) number says the priority, delay budget and loss target - voice gets a better one than web browsing. A private APN with the wrong name or no subscription in the core is a classic "registered, no data" cause: the core rejects the session with a cause code that's only visible in the modem's log.		设备可同时拥有多个会话：互联网数据、用于 VoLTE 的 IMS（语音本身有保证比特率承载）、公司路由器的私有 APN。各自有独立地址，也可连接不同网关。每条流的 QoS 由 QCI（4G）或 5QI（5G）指定优先级、延迟预算和丢包目标，语音通常优于浏览网页。私有 APN 名称错误或核心网未开通权限，常导致“已注册却无数据”；拒绝原因码通常只能在调制解调器日志中看到。
What is an APN (4G) or DNN (5G)?		APN（4G）或 DNN（5G）是什么？
The name of the data network a device asks to connect to		设备请求连接的数据网络名称
The phone's serial number		手机序列号
The tower's name		基站名称
The MME signals; the gateways carry your data.		MME 处理信令，网关承载数据。
The MME authenticates you and tracks which area you're in. The SGW carries your data from the tower; the PGW gives you an IP address and connects to the internet (the APN). The HSS holds subscriber data.		MME 验证身份并跟踪所在区域。SGW 从基站承接数据；PGW 分配 IP 并按 APN 接入互联网。HSS 保存用户资料。
initial UE message (attach request)		初始 UE 消息（附着请求）
An MME problem means phones can't attach at all. A PGW or APN problem means attached but no data - the bars look fine.		MME 故障可能使手机完全无法附着。PGW 或 APN 故障则表现为已附着但无数据，信号格仍正常。
Signalling goes eNodeB → MME (S1-MME, protocol S1AP with NAS inside). Data goes in GTP-U tunnels: eNodeB → SGW (S1-U) → PGW (S5/S8) → internet (SGi). Each tunnel has an ID (TEID), so a capture on S1-U shows your packets wrapped in GTP. The MME asks the HSS for your keys and profile (S6a, Diameter); the PCRF sets policy and the bearers' QoS. When you roam, the PGW can be your home network's - your traffic goes home first, which is why roaming can be slow.		信令从 eNodeB 经 S1-MME 到 MME，使用 S1AP 承载 NAS。数据走 GTP-U 隧道：eNodeB 经 S1-U 到 SGW，再经 S5/S8 到 PGW，最后经 SGi 到互联网。每条隧道有 TEID，因此 S1-U 抓包会看到 GTP 封装。MME 通过 S6a Diameter 向 HSS 获取密钥和资料，PCRF 设定策略和承载 QoS。漫游时 PGW 可能仍在归属网络，数据先绕回本国，因此漫游可能更慢。
In a 4G core, which part handles signalling and mobility (not your data)?		4G 核心网中，哪个组件处理信令和移动性，而非你的数据？
The MME		MME
The PGW		PGW
The eNodeB		eNodeB
The DNS server		DNS 服务器
Your phone, the radio towers, and a core network that decides who you are and where your data goes.		手机、基站，以及决定你是谁、数据发往哪里的核心网。
The phone (UE) talks by radio to a base station (eNodeB in 4G, gNodeB in 5G). The base stations connect over fibre to the core, which checks your SIM, gives you an IP address and sends your data to the internet.		手机 UE 通过无线电连接基站，4G 叫 eNodeB，5G 叫 gNodeB。基站经光纤连接核心网，核心网验证 SIM、分配 IP，并将数据送往互联网。
Tower		基站
radio connection		无线连接
Full bars and no internet usually means the data session (APN/DNN) failed, not the radio. Slow data with full bars is often a busy cell - many users sharing it.		信号满格却无法上网，通常是 APN/DNN 数据会话失败，不是无线信号问题。满格但网速慢，则常是基站繁忙、多人共享。
The radio access network (RAN) handles the air: scheduling who transmits when, handovers between cells, signal quality. The core does everything else: authentication (from the SIM's secret key), mobility (which tower you're under), sessions (your IP address and the path for your data), policy and charging. Control (signalling) and user data travel separately: signalling to the core's control functions, your packets through user-plane gateways. "Registered but no data" is a classic case where the control side worked and the data session didn't. A cellular router or a phone hotspot looks like any other router to your PC - AdeeboMeter sees the link to it, DNS and reachability, not the radio or the core.		无线接入网 RAN 负责无线调度、基站间切换和信号质量。核心网负责 SIM 密钥认证、位置管理、IP 与数据路径会话、策略和计费。控制信令与用户数据分开传输，信令交给控制功能，数据包经过用户面网关。“已注册但无法上网”常意味着控制面成功，数据会话失败。对电脑来说，蜂窝路由器或手机热点与普通路由器相似；AdeeboMeter 能看到到它的连接、DNS 和可达性，看不到无线接入网或核心网内部。
What are the three big parts of a mobile network?		移动网络的三大部分是什么？
The phone, the radio access network (towers), and the core network		手机、无线接入网（基站）和核心网
Wi-Fi, Bluetooth and cable		Wi-Fi、蓝牙和网线
The SIM, the screen and the battery		SIM 卡、屏幕和电池
DNS, DHCP and NAT		DNS、DHCP 和 NAT
Non-standalone borrows the 4G core; standalone is 5G all the way.		非独立组网借用 4G 核心网，独立组网则全程采用 5G。
NSA adds 5G radio to a 4G anchor cell: the phone signals over 4G and gets extra speed on 5G, but the core is still the 4G one. SA uses a 5G core and 5G for everything.		NSA 在 4G 锚点小区基础上增加 5G 无线资源，手机经 4G 传信令，用 5G 提速，但核心仍是 4G。SA 则使用 5G 核心网和全套 5G 接入。
4G anchor cell		4G 锚点小区
attach (as in 4G)		附着，过程同 4G
A device that only supports SA in a place that only has NSA (or the reverse) falls back to 4G - working, slower.		仅支持 SA 的设备遇到只有 NSA 的网络，或反过来，通常会回退到 4G，能用但更慢。
NSA (option 3x) gave early 5G its speed without a new core. Features that need the 5G core - network slicing, the lower-latency user plane, RedCap devices, the newer session handling - only come with SA. On NSA, losing the 4G anchor drops the 5G leg too, so a "5G" icon can flicker while data stalls. Routers usually show which one they're on (EN-DC means NSA). Latency on NSA is close to good 4G; on SA with a nearby UPF it can be lower.		NSA 的 option 3x 让早期 5G 无需新核心网就能提速。网络切片、更低延迟用户面、RedCap 设备和新会话机制等依赖 5G 核心网的功能，需要 SA。NSA 中若失去 4G 锚点，5G 链路也会断，因此可能出现 5G 图标闪动、数据停顿。路由器通常会显示模式，EN-DC 表示 NSA。NSA 延迟接近良好的 4G，SA 配合附近 UPF 时则可能更低。
In 5G non-standalone (NSA), which core is used?		5G 非独立组网 NSA 使用哪个核心网？
The 4G core (EPC)		4G 核心网（EPC）
The 5G core		5G 核心网
No core at all		不需要核心网
The Wi-Fi router		Wi-Fi 路由器
eNodeB and gNodeB - the towers		eNodeB 与 gNodeB：基站
The base station: radio on one side, fibre to the core on the other.		基站一侧是无线电，另一侧通过光纤接入核心网。
eNodeB is the 4G base station, gNodeB the 5G one. They schedule the radio, measure your signal, hand you to a better cell, and connect to the core over S1 (4G) or N2 and N3 (5G).		eNodeB 是 4G 基站，gNodeB 是 5G 基站。它们调度无线资源、测量信号、切换到更优小区，并通过 S1（4G）或 N2、N3（5G）连接核心网。
random access - I'd like to talk		随机接入：我想通信
radio resources granted		已分配无线资源
measurement report - neighbour cell is stronger		测量报告：邻区信号更强
handover command - move to the neighbour cell		切换命令：转到邻区
Good RSRP but poor SINR means interference or a busy cell, not distance. Constant handovers at a cell edge show up as short stalls and jitter.		RSRP 良好但 SINR 差，提示干扰或小区繁忙，而非距离太远。小区边缘频繁切换会表现为短暂停顿和抖动。
Each cell broadcasts its identity; the phone measures signal strength (RSRP) and quality (RSRQ, SINR) and reports them, and the base station decides handovers - directly over the X2/Xn link to the neighbour cell when it can. A 5G gNodeB is often split: a radio unit on the mast, distributed and central units further back (CU/DU split, O-RAN). Early 5G is "non-standalone" (NSA): 5G radio added to a 4G eNodeB, still on the 4G core. "Standalone" (SA) uses the 5G core. RedCap is a lighter 5G for sensors and routers that don't need full speed.		每个小区广播身份。手机测量并上报信号强度 RSRP 和质量 RSRQ、SINR，由基站决定切换，条件允许时通过 X2/Xn 直接联系邻站。5G gNodeB 常拆分为塔上的无线单元，以及后方的分布式与集中式单元，即 CU/DU 拆分和 O-RAN。早期非独立组网 NSA 在 4G eNodeB 上增加 5G 无线，仍用 4G 核心网；独立组网 SA 使用 5G 核心网。RedCap 是面向传感器和低速率路由器的轻量 5G。
What is a gNodeB?		gNodeB 是什么？
The 5G base station (the "tower" equipment)		5G 基站设备
A type of SIM card		一种 SIM 卡
The 5G core's database		5G 核心网数据库
A Wi-Fi router		Wi-Fi 路由器
Each one starts the next - and if certain ones die, Windows dies with them.		一个启动下一个，某些核心进程退出时 Windows 也会停止。
Power on to desktop: firmware, Windows' loader, the kernel, then the core processes in order.		从通电到桌面：固件、Windows 加载程序、内核，再依次启动核心进程。
Firmware		固件
Boot manager		启动管理器
load Windows		加载 Windows
If one of the core processes dies, Windows stops with a blue screen.		核心进程异常终止时，Windows 会蓝屏停止。
Why does Windows stop if certain core processes die?		某些核心进程停止后，Windows 为什么也会停止？
Each one starts the next, and Windows can't run without them		它们依次启动，Windows 缺少它们就无法运行
They hold your files		它们保存着你的文件
They are viruses		它们是病毒
They run the internet		它们控制互联网
csrss.exe - the session's core		csrss.exe：会话核心
Old name, still essential.		名字虽老，仍然不可或缺。
Draws console windows and helps start and shut down every program.		绘制控制台窗口，并协助各程序启动和关闭。
start for the session		为会话启动
Can't be ended; if it dies, Windows stops. Two copies is normal.		不能结束它，否则 Windows 会停止。存在两个实例很正常。
What happens if csrss.exe stops?		csrss.exe 停止后会怎样？
Windows stops - it's essential to the session		Windows 停止，因为它是会话必需组件
Only the sound stops		只有声音停止
Nothing, it's optional		没影响，它是可选组件
The taskbar restarts		任务栏重新启动
lsass.exe - the security guard		lsass.exe：安全卫士
Checks every password and keeps your sign-in tokens.		检查密码，保存登录令牌。
The security guard: checks your password or PIN and keeps your sign-in tokens.		安全卫士：验证密码或 PIN，保存登录令牌。
check this PIN		验证此 PIN
correct - here's your token		正确，这是你的令牌
Never end it - Windows restarts within a minute. High CPU at work is usually sign-in traffic.		切勿结束它，Windows 会在一分钟内重启。办公环境中 CPU 高通常与登录请求有关。
What does lsass.exe do?		lsass.exe 做什么？
Checks sign-ins and keeps your sign-in tokens		验证登录并保存登录令牌
Draws windows		绘制窗口
Plays sound		播放声音
Indexes files for search		为文件建立搜索索引
svchost.exe - one host, many services		svchost.exe：一个宿主，多个服务
Why there are twenty of them, and what's inside each.		为什么有二十多个，每个里面又是什么。
A shared host: many small Windows services run inside each svchost.exe.		共享服务宿主：每个 svchost.exe 内可运行多个小型 Windows 服务。
host these services		承载这些服务
When 'svchost' is busy, it's one service inside - Inside Windows shows which.		svchost 繁忙时，是其中某个服务在忙。“Windows 内部”可查看具体服务。
Why are there many svchost.exe processes?		为什么会有很多 svchost.exe 进程？
Each hosts one or more Windows services		每个承载一个或多个 Windows 服务
They are copies of a virus		它们是病毒的副本
One per browser tab		每个浏览器标签页一个
One per user		每位用户一个
wininit.exe - starts the background world		wininit.exe：启动后台系统

start		启动
Can't be ended; if it dies, Windows stops.		不能结束它，否则 Windows 会停止。

Your browser		你的浏览器
The printer		打印机
winlogon.exe - your sign-in		winlogon.exe：管理登录

Handles signing in, locking (Win+L) and Ctrl+Alt+Del.		处理登录、锁定（Win+L）和 Ctrl+Alt+Del。
You		你
If sign-in hangs, a startup program or a profile problem is usually behind it.		登录卡住通常与启动程序或用户配置问题有关。

Drawing the desktop		绘制桌面
Downloading updates		下载更新
dwm.exe - the desktop's painter		dwm.exe：桌面画师
Every window you see is drawn by it.		你看到的每个窗口都由它合成。
Draws everything you see - windows, shadows, animations - with the graphics card.		利用显卡绘制你看到的窗口、阴影和动画。
here's my window		这是我的窗口
High GPU or memory here usually means many monitors or a graphics driver issue.		这里 GPU 或内存占用高，通常与多显示器或显卡驱动问题有关。

Draws (composes) every window you see		绘制、合成你看到的每个窗口
Checks passwords		验证密码
Downloads updates		下载更新
Runs scheduled tasks		运行计划任务
explorer.exe - your desktop, taskbar and folders		explorer.exe：桌面、任务栏与文件夹
The shell: everything you click to start things.		系统外壳，你点击启动内容的主要界面。

Taskbar frozen? Restart 'Windows Explorer' in Task Manager - nothing is lost.		任务栏卡住？在任务管理器中重启“Windows 资源管理器”，不会丢失内容。

A virus scanner		病毒扫描器

services.exe - the Service Control Manager		services.exe：服务控制管理器
Starts, stops and watches every Windows service.		启动、停止并监控所有 Windows 服务。
The service manager: starts, stops and restarts every Windows service.		服务管理器：启动、停止和重启每项 Windows 服务。
A service that keeps restarting shows up here as repeated starts - check its name.		服务反复重启时，这里会显示多次启动，请检查其名称。

Starting, stopping and watching Windows services		Windows 服务的启动、停止与监控
Your passwords		你的密码
The internet connection speed		互联网连接速度
The screen brightness		屏幕亮度
AES - one key locks and unlocks		AES：同一把密钥加密和解密
The fast lock that carries all your data.		承载所有数据的快速加密锁。
AES uses one shared key: the same key locks and unlocks. It is fast enough to encrypt everything you send.		AES 使用共享密钥，同一把密钥负责加密和解密，速度足以加密你发送的全部内容。
AES-locked data (key K)		AES 加密数据（密钥 K）
unlock with the same key K		用同一密钥 K 解密
AES-locked answer (key K)		AES 加密应答（密钥 K）
The hard part isn't the lock - it's getting the same key to both sides without anyone seeing it. That's what the key agreement solves.		难点不在锁，而是如何不让旁人看到，却让双方获得相同密钥。这由密钥协商解决。
AES-GCM also seals the data, so any change on the way is caught. A 128-bit key already has more combinations than anyone could ever try; 256-bit is for the long haul.		AES-GCM 还能验证完整性，发现传输中的任何改动。128 比特密钥的组合已多到无法穷举，256 比特则为长期安全留出余量。
In AES, what unlocks the data?		AES 使用什么解密数据？
The same key that locked it		加密时使用的同一把密钥
The server's certificate		服务器证书
Any long password		任意长密码
The website's address		网站地址
A certificate - the server's passport		证书：服务器的护照
Name, photo (the public key), dates, and the issuer's stamp.		姓名、照片（公钥）、有效日期和签发机构盖章。
A certificate ties a name (www.example.com) to a public key, with start and end dates, signed by an issuer that vouches for it.		证书将 www.example.com 这样的名称与公钥绑定，注明有效起止日期，并由签发机构签名担保。
Issuer		签发机构
I checked you own www.example.com - here's your signed passport		已确认你拥有 www.example.com，这是签名后的护照
Wrong name, past its end date, or an issuer your PC doesn't trust: the padlock breaks. Toolbox › HTTPS check says which.		名称不符、过期或签发机构不受电脑信任，都会让安全锁失效。工具箱 › HTTPS 检查可指出原因。
The names it is valid for are in the SAN list. A site's own certificate says CA: false; only issuers say CA: true.		证书适用的名称在 SAN 列表中。网站证书标记 CA: false，只有签发机构证书才标记 CA: true。
Who vouches for a certificate?		谁为证书担保？
Its issuer, a certificate authority		签发它的证书颁发机构
The website itself, always		总是网站自己
Your internet provider		你的网络运营商
Diffie-Hellman - mixing paint in public		Diffie-Hellman：公开混合颜料
Two strangers agree on a secret while everyone listens.		两个陌生人即使被旁听，也能协商共同秘密。
Both start with a public colour, add their own secret colour and swap the mixes. Each adds its secret again - both get the same final colour; a listener can't unmix it.		双方先用公开颜色，各自加入秘密颜色并交换混合结果，再加入自己的秘密颜色，最后得到相同颜色。旁听者无法把混合颜料拆开。
public yellow + my secret red = orange		公开黄色 + 我的秘密红色 = 橙色
public yellow + my secret blue = green		公开黄色 + 我的秘密蓝色 = 绿色
green + my red = brown		绿色 + 我的红色 = 棕色
orange + my blue = the same brown		橙色 + 我的蓝色 = 同样的棕色
On its own it doesn't prove who you're talking to - the certificate does that. ECDHE makes a fresh secret every session (forward secrecy).		它本身不能证明对方身份，这需要证书。ECDHE 为每次会话生成新秘密，提供前向保密。
What do both sides end up with after Diffie-Hellman?		Diffie-Hellman 完成后，双方得到什么？
The same secret, never sent across		从未直接传输过的相同秘密
Each other's password		对方的密码
The website's IP address		网站 IP 地址
A hash - a file's fingerprint		哈希：文件的指纹
Change one bit, get a completely different fingerprint.		改动一比特，就会得到完全不同的指纹。
A hash squeezes any file into a short fingerprint (SHA-256: 64 hex digits). The same file always gives the same one.		哈希将任意文件压缩成短指纹，SHA-256 为 64 位十六进制字符。同一文件始终得到同一指纹。
Download page		下载页面
setup.exe is 3f2a...9c1d		setup.exe 的哈希为 3f2a...9c1d
A download whose hash doesn't match the published one is damaged or not the real file - get it again.		下载文件的哈希与公布值不符，说明损坏或不是原文件，请重新下载。
You can't turn a fingerprint back into the file, and nobody can make two different files with the same fingerprint (with SHA-256 - MD5 and SHA-1 are broken for that: someone can make a matching pair, though not a match for a file that already exists). Certificates, Windows Update and package managers all lean on this.		无法从指纹还原文件，对于 SHA-256，也无法实际构造两个不同文件具有同一指纹。MD5 和 SHA-1 的抗碰撞能力已被攻破，可构造碰撞文件对，但这不等于能匹配任意已有文件。证书、Windows Update 和软件包管理器都依赖哈希。
You change one letter in a 2 GB file. What happens to its SHA-256 fingerprint?		修改一个 2 GB 文件中的一个字母，SHA-256 指纹会怎样？
It changes completely		完全改变
It changes by one letter too		也只改变一个字母
It stays the same		保持不变
It gets shorter		变短
Squeezes memory instead of writing it to disk.		压缩内存，而非写入磁盘。
Windows squeezes memory it isn't using right now instead of writing it to disk.		Windows 将暂时不用的内存压缩，而非写入磁盘。
Memory compression		内存压缩
squeeze this idle app		压缩这个空闲应用
Large means memory is tight - close something heavy or add RAM.		占用较大说明内存紧张，可关闭高占用应用或增加 RAM。
What does Memory Compression do?		内存压缩做什么？
Squeezes memory instead of writing it to the disk		压缩内存，而非写入磁盘
Deletes old files		删除旧文件
Makes downloads smaller		缩小下载文件
Speeds up Wi-Fi		加快 Wi-Fi
It's rarely the processor - it's memory and the disk.		通常不是处理器，而是内存和磁盘。
When memory is full, Windows parks parts of programs on the disk and reads them back when needed - thousands of times slower.		内存满时，Windows 将程序的一部分暂存到磁盘，需要时再读回，速度会慢上数千倍。
I need my code		我需要我的代码
carry on		继续
Many pages read back per second, or a disk taking more than 15 ms per request, makes everything stutter.		每秒大量页面读回，或磁盘单次请求超过 15 ms，都会让操作卡顿。
What usually makes a PC feel slow?		什么通常会让电脑感觉慢？
Running out of memory, so it waits on the disk		内存不足，导致等待磁盘读取
A slow processor clock		处理器时钟慢
A small screen		屏幕小
Too many desktop icons		桌面图标太多
msedgewebview2.exe - Edge inside other apps		msedgewebview2.exe：其他应用内的 Edge
Why Teams and new Outlook have so many Edge processes.		为什么 Teams 和新版 Outlook 有这么多 Edge 进程。
A built-in Edge engine that apps like Teams, Outlook and widgets use to draw their screens.		Teams、Outlook 和小组件等应用用来绘制界面的内嵌 Edge 引擎。
draw the chat window		绘制聊天窗口
drawn		已绘制
Many copies are normal - each belongs to an app; Inside Windows shows which.		多个实例很正常，每个属于某个应用。“Windows 内部”可查看对应关系。
Why do Teams and new Outlook run msedgewebview2.exe?		Teams 和新版 Outlook 为什么运行 msedgewebview2.exe？
They show their screens using Edge's engine inside the app		它们使用应用内的 Edge 引擎显示界面
They are hiding a browser		它们隐藏了一个浏览器
They mine cryptocurrency		它们在挖加密货币
They update Windows		它们在更新 Windows
MsMpEng.exe - Microsoft Defender		MsMpEng.exe：Microsoft Defender
Checks every file as it's opened.		打开文件时逐个检查。
Microsoft Defender's scanner - checks files as they open and runs scans.		Microsoft Defender 扫描引擎，打开文件时检查，并执行扫描。
Program		程序
open this file		打开此文件
Busy during big copies or builds; never stop it - it's your antivirus.		大量复制或构建时会繁忙。不要停止它，它是你的防病毒保护。
What does MsMpEng.exe (Defender) do as files are opened?		打开文件时，MsMpEng.exe（Defender）做什么？

OneDrive - files on demand		OneDrive：文件按需下载
Your files in the cloud, downloaded only when you open them.		文件保存在云端，仅打开时下载。
Keeps a folder on your PC in sync with the cloud, both ways, in the background.		在后台双向同步本机文件夹与云端。
uploading your photo		正在上传你的照片
A big sync uses your upload - that's when calls get worse.		大量同步占用上传带宽，此时通话容易变差。
What does 'files on demand' mean in OneDrive?		OneDrive 的“文件按需”是什么意思？
Files are listed but only downloaded when you open them		先列出文件，仅在打开时下载
Files are deleted when not used		不用的文件会删除
Files are emailed to you		文件通过邮件发给你
Files are printed		文件会被打印
Signs in once, keeps a copy on your PC, syncs in the background.		登录一次，在本机保留副本，并在后台同步。
Outlook keeps a copy of your mailbox on the PC and syncs it with Exchange Online over HTTPS.		Outlook 在本机保存邮箱副本，并通过 HTTPS 与 Exchange Online 同步。
Sign-in (WAM)		登录（WAM）
token please		请提供令牌
token		令牌
Slow Outlook is often the big local mailbox file or OneDrive using your upload.		Outlook 慢常常是本地邮箱文件太大，或 OneDrive 正在占用上传带宽。
Why can Outlook show your mail while offline?		Outlook 为什么能离线显示邮件？
It keeps a copy on your PC and syncs in the background		它在本机保留副本，并在后台同步
It guesses the mail		它猜测邮件内容
The mail is stored in DNS		邮件保存在 DNS 中
It can't		它不能离线显示
Hand out the padlock, keep the only key.		公开挂锁，自己保管唯一的钥匙。
A key pair: anyone may have the public key; only the owner has the private key. What one half does, only the other half can undo.		密钥对中，公钥可交给任何人，私钥仅由拥有者保管。一部分完成的特定操作，需要另一部分才能验证或解开。
here's my public key		这是我的公钥
(checks against the public key)		使用公钥验证
only my private key can do this part		这一步只有我的私钥能完成
A leaked private key means anyone can pretend to be you - the certificate has to be revoked and replaced.		私钥泄漏意味着别人可能冒充你，需要撤销并更换证书。
RSA and ECDSA are the two common kinds. ECDSA keys are much smaller for the same strength (P-256 is about RSA 3072).		常见类型为 RSA 和 ECDSA。同等安全强度下 ECDSA 密钥更小，P-256 大致相当于 RSA 3072。
Which half of a key pair must stay secret?		密钥对的哪一部分必须保密？
The private key		私钥
The public key		公钥
Neither		都不用
RuntimeBroker.exe - the permission checker		RuntimeBroker.exe：权限检查员
Asks Windows' permission on behalf of Store apps.		代表商店应用向 Windows 请求权限。
Checks Store apps' permissions - camera, location, files.		检查商店应用的摄像头、位置和文件权限。
Store app		商店应用
may I use the camera?		我可以使用摄像头吗？

Your internet speed		你的网速
Your files for viruses		文件是否感染病毒
Your sign-in password		你的登录密码
SearchHost.exe / SearchIndexer - Windows Search		SearchHost.exe / SearchIndexer：Windows 搜索
The search box, and the index behind it.		搜索框及其背后的索引。

A list of websites		网站列表
A backup of your PC		电脑备份
A map of your network		网络地图
A signature - sign with private, check with public		数字签名：私钥签名，公钥验证
Proves who wrote it, and that nobody changed it.		证明由谁签署，并确认内容未被修改。
The signer hashes the document and seals the hash with their private key. Anyone checks it with the signer's public key.		签名者计算文档哈希，再用私钥签名。任何人都能用签名者的公钥验证。
Signer		签名者
hash the document, seal it with my private key		计算文档哈希，再用我的私钥签名
document + signature		文档 + 签名
open the seal with the public key - the hash matches		用公钥验证签名，哈希一致
Change one bit of the document and the check fails - that's the point. "Bad signature" means altered, or signed by someone else.		文档改动一比特，验证就会失败，这正是签名的作用。“签名无效”表示内容被修改，或签名者不符。
How do you check a digital signature?		如何验证数字签名？
With the signer's public key		使用签名者的公钥
With the signer's private key		使用签名者的私钥
With your own password		使用自己的密码
With the file's name		使用文件名称
The engine room - drivers and the kernel itself.		系统动力核心：驱动与内核本身。
The Windows kernel and drivers - the part that talks to the hardware.		Windows 内核与驱动，负责与硬件通信。
Wi-Fi card		Wi-Fi 网卡
packet arrived		收到数据包
High CPU here usually means a driver: Wi-Fi, storage or antivirus filter.		这里 CPU 高通常与驱动有关，例如 Wi-Fi、存储或防病毒过滤驱动。
What runs inside the System process?		System 进程中运行什么？
The Windows kernel and drivers		Windows 内核与驱动
Sign in, signal, find a path, then audio and video over UDP.		登录、发出呼叫信令、寻找路径，再通过 UDP 传输音视频。
Teams signs in, rings the other side over HTTPS, then sends audio and video over UDP.		Teams 先登录，通过 HTTPS 呼叫对方，再通过 UDP 发送音视频。
Robot voice or freezing: UDP blocked, the VPN in the way, or weak Wi-Fi.		机械音或画面冻结可能与 UDP 被阻止、VPN 干扰或 Wi-Fi 信号弱有关。
How does a Teams call prefer to carry audio and video?		Teams 通话优先用什么传输音视频？
UDP, falling back to TCP if UDP is blocked		UDP，被阻止时回退到 TCP
Email		电子邮件
Only TCP port 25		仅 TCP 端口 25
Sign-in broker (WAM) - one sign-in for every Microsoft app		登录代理（WAM）：一次登录供所有 Microsoft 应用使用
Why Teams, Outlook and OneDrive never ask for your password separately.		为什么 Teams、Outlook 和 OneDrive 不需要分别索要密码。
Windows' sign-in broker: Office and Teams ask it for tokens instead of asking you for passwords.		Windows 登录代理：Office 和 Teams 向它请求令牌，而不是向你索要密码。
Endless sign-in prompts: the token broker is stuck - sign out of Office and back in.		反复要求登录，可能是令牌代理卡住。可尝试退出 Office 后重新登录。
Why don't Teams, Outlook and OneDrive each ask for your password?		Teams、Outlook 和 OneDrive 为什么不分别要求输入密码？
They share one sign-in through Windows' sign-in broker		它们通过 Windows 登录代理共享一次登录
They don't need one		它们不需要密码
They store it in plain text		它们以明文保存密码
They use DNS to sign in		它们使用 DNS 登录
A wrong clock breaks the padlock before the network does.		系统时间错误，可能比网络问题更早让安全锁失效。
Certificates have a start and an end date. Your PC checks them against its own clock - so a clock that's far off fails every site at once.		证书有生效和到期日期，电脑根据本地时间验证。时间差得太远，会让所有网站同时验证失败。
my certificate, valid Nov 2026 - Nov 2027		我的证书，有效期为 2026 年 11 月至 2027 年 11 月
my clock says 2000 - not valid yet		电脑时间为 2000 年，尚未生效
All sites showing certificate errors at the same time? Check the clock first (Toolbox › Clock check).		所有网站同时报证书错误？先检查时间（工具箱 › 时间检查）。
Your PC thinks it's the year 2000. What happens to HTTPS?		电脑认为当前是 2000 年，HTTPS 会怎样？
Every certificate looks "not yet valid"		所有证书都可能显示“尚未生效”
Pages load faster		网页加载更快
Nothing - the clock doesn't matter		没影响，时间不重要
Only video breaks		只有视频出问题
A plan for every dollar before the month spends it for you.		在每个月自然花光钱之前，先规划每一笔资金。
A simple start is 50/30/20: about half of take-home pay for needs (rent, food, bills), 30% for wants, 20% for saving and paying off debt. Pay yourself first: move the saving on payday, not at the end of the month.		简单的起点是 50/30/20：税后收入约一半用于房租、食物、账单等必要开支，30% 用于个人想要的消费，20% 用于储蓄和还债。发薪时先划出储蓄，而非等到月底。
High-interest debt first: paying off a 20% card beats almost any investment.		高息债务优先：偿还年息 20% 的信用卡，通常比大多数投资收益更划算。
In the 50/30/20 rule, the 20 is for...		50/30/20 法则中的 20 用于……
Saving and paying off debt		储蓄和偿还债务
Rent		房租
Holidays		度假
Taxes		税款
Up 20% from a low is a bull; down 20% from a high is a bear.		较低点上涨 20% 称牛市，较高点下跌 20% 称熊市。
In a bull market prices are rising and people feel confident. A bear market is a fall of 20% or more from the last high - usually with plenty of fear in the news.		牛市中价格上涨，市场信心较强。熊市指较最近高点下跌 20% 或更多，新闻中通常也充满担忧。
Bears have always ended so far, but some took years to recover - money you need soon doesn't belong in shares.		截至目前熊市最终都会结束，但部分复苏耗时多年，因此近期需要的资金不宜承受股票波动。
A bear market means prices have...		熊市通常意味着价格……
Fallen 20% or more from their last high		较最近高点下跌 20% 或更多
Risen 20% from a low		较低点上涨 20%
Stayed flat for a year		一年没有变化
Fallen 1% in a day		一天内下跌 1%
Growth on your growth - slow at first, then fast.		收益继续产生收益，起初慢，后来快。
Money that grows earns on what it already earned. At 6% a year it roughly doubles every 12 years (the rule of 72: 72 ÷ 6 = 12). Time matters more than the amount: starting ten years earlier can beat putting in twice as much later.		增长后的资金继续产生收益。年增长 6% 时约 12 年翻倍，72 法则为 72 ÷ 6 = 12。时间很重要，早开始十年，结果可能胜过晚些时候投入两倍。
Compounding works on debt too: a credit card at 20% doubles what you owe in under 4 years.		债务也会复利增长：年利率 20% 的信用卡欠款，不到 4 年就可能翻倍。
With the rule of 72, money growing 8% a year doubles in about...		按 72 法则，资金每年增长 8%，大约多久翻倍？
9 years		9 年
72 years		72 年
8 years		8 年
2 years		2 年
The network carries the order; it doesn't fill it.		网络负责传递订单，不负责成交。
Your order travels from your PC through Wi-Fi, your router and your provider to the broker. AdeeboMeter times that path every few seconds while the market is open and says whose side a slow moment is on.		订单从电脑经 Wi-Fi、路由器和运营商到达券商。市场开放时，AdeeboMeter 每隔几秒测量这条路径，指出变慢发生在哪一侧。
Buy (2 ms)		买入（2 ms）
If the order screen spins after a lost reply, check your orders list before clicking again - a second click can mean a second order.		丢失应答后，如果订单页面一直转圈，再次点击前先检查订单列表，第二次点击可能产生第二笔订单。
What can AdeeboMeter's broker check NOT tell you?		AdeeboMeter 的券商检查不能告诉你什么？
How fast the broker actually fills your order		券商实际成交订单的速度
Your delay to the broker's server		你到券商服务器的延迟
Whether your Wi-Fi is dropping		Wi-Fi 是否断线
Whether your provider is losing packets		运营商是否丢包
Know which clock your number is on.		先了解数值对应的时间。
Check the time stamp on any price you trade on: brokers can take orders while showing delayed quotes, and live data is often a subscription. Free sites often show prices about 15 minutes late. AdeeboMeter's Trading summary uses end-of-day numbers, after the close.		使用报价前要查看时间戳：券商可能显示延迟行情，却仍接受订单；实时数据往往需要订阅。免费网站常延迟约 15 分钟。AdeeboMeter 交易摘要使用收盘后的日终数据。
Broker		券商
live price		实时价格
Comparing a live price with a delayed one makes it look like something jumped - check the time stamp first.		将实时价与延迟价比较，会误以为突然跳涨，先核对时间戳。
Prices on many free websites are usually...		很多免费网站的报价通常是……
Delayed by about 15 minutes		延迟约 15 分钟
Live to the second		精确到秒的实时价格
From yesterday only		只有昨天的数据
Made up		虚构数据
A 10% fall is normal weather, not a storm.		10% 下跌像常见天气变化，不一定是风暴。
A correction is a fall of 10% or more from a high. Historically, broad markets have had one roughly every year or two, and most recovered within months.		回调指较高点下跌 10% 或更多。历史上广泛市场大致每一两年经历一次，多数在数月内恢复。
Selling in a dip turns a paper loss into a real one - and you then have to guess when to get back in.		在下跌中卖出，会将账面亏损变成已实现亏损，之后还得判断何时重新进入。
A correction is usually defined as a fall of...		回调通常定义为下跌……
10% or more from a recent high		较近期高点下跌 10% 或更多
Exactly 1%		恰好 1%
50% or more		50% 或更多
Any red day		任何收跌的交易日
One company can sink; a basket rarely does.		单家公司可能陷入困境，一篮子公司通常更稳。
Holding many companies (an index fund holds hundreds) means one bad company barely dents the whole. Spreading across countries and types of investment smooths things further.		持有多家公司，例如含数百家公司的指数基金，意味着一家公司的问题对整体影响较小。跨国家和资产类别分散，可进一步平滑波动。
Owning five tech companies isn't spread - they tend to fall together.		持有五家科技公司不等于充分分散，因为它们往往一起下跌。
Why does an index fund usually swing less than one company's shares?		指数基金为什么通常比单家公司股票波动小？
Many companies average each other out		多家公司的表现相互平衡
It's guaranteed by the government		政府保证它安全
It only holds banks		它只持有银行
It never falls		它从不下跌
Cash set aside so a surprise doesn't become a disaster.		预留现金，避免意外支出变成财务危机。
Keep about 3-6 months of essential costs in a safe, easy-to-reach account. When the car breaks or a job ends, you pay from the fund instead of a credit card - or instead of selling investments at a bad moment.		将约 3-6 个月必要开支放在安全、易取用的账户中。汽车故障或失业时，可用它支付，而非借信用卡或在不利时机卖出投资。
Without one, the worst surprises tend to arrive together: a recession can cost you your job and push prices down at the same time.		没有应急资金时，坏事可能一起来：经济衰退可能同时带来失业和资产价格下跌。
An emergency fund is usually about...		应急资金通常约为……
3-6 months of essential costs, kept safe		稳妥存放的 3-6 个月必要开支
All your money, invested in shares		将所有钱投入股票
One week of costs		一周开支
Whatever is left after a holiday		度假后剩下的钱
A small yearly fee eats a big slice over decades.		每年看似很小的费用，几十年后会占去很大一部分。
A fee is taken every year from the whole amount, so it compounds against you. Over 30 years at 6% growth, a 2% yearly fee leaves about 40% less than a 0.2% fee does.		费用每年从全部资产中扣除，也会产生复利影响。按年增长 6% 计算，30 年后，2% 年费比 0.2% 年费留下的资金约少 40%。
Fees hide in names like MER and management expense - look for the percentage.		费用可能叫 MER 或管理费，请查看百分比。
Over 30 years, a 2% yearly fee instead of 0.2% leaves you with roughly...		30 年中，年费从 0.2% 变为 2%，最终资金大约会……
40% less		少 40%
2% less		少 2%
the same		相同
twice as much		翻倍
A US stock in Canada is two bets: the stock and the dollar.		在加拿大持有美股，同时受股票与美元汇率影响。
Anything priced in another currency moves with the exchange rate too. Banks and apps also add a spread - the difference between their rate and the daily rate - which is a fee.		以外币计价的资产也随汇率变化。银行和应用还可能收取点差，即其报价与当日参考汇率之差，这也是费用。
Bank		银行
change 1,000 CAD to USD		将 1,000 CAD 换为 USD
at 1.38 (daily rate 1.35)		汇率 1.38（当日参考值 1.35）
the 0.03 per dollar is the fee		每美元的 0.03 差额就是费用
"No-fee" conversions often hide the fee in a worse rate. Compare with the day's rate in Trading.		“免手续费”换汇常把费用藏在较差的汇率中，可与交易页面的当日汇率比较。
You own a US stock that stays flat, but the US dollar falls against the Canadian dollar. In CAD your holding...		你持有的美股价格不变，但美元相对加元下跌，以 CAD 计算的持仓会……
Is worth less		价值下降
Is worth more		价值上升
Stays exactly the same		完全不变
Doubles		翻倍
The same $100 buys a little less each year.		同样的 $100，每年能买到的东西更少。
Inflation is prices rising over time. The Bank of Canada aims for about 2% a year. At 3%, $100 of groceries today costs about $134 in ten years - so $100 in a drawer quietly buys about a quarter less.		通胀是价格随时间上升。加拿大银行目标约为每年 2%。按 3% 计算，今天 $100 的食品十年后约需 $134，因此抽屉里的 $100 会悄悄损失约四分之一购买力。
Cash feels safe because the number never drops - but at 3% inflation it loses half its buying power in about 24 years.		现金金额不下降，看似安全，但在 3% 通胀下，约 24 年就损失一半购买力。
At 3% inflation, $100 kept as cash for 10 years buys about...		通胀率 3% 时，持有 $100 现金 10 年后的购买力约为……
$74 worth of today's goods		今天价值 $74 的商品
$100 worth		价值 $100
$130 worth		价值 $130
$3 less		少 $3
More buyers than sellers - for whatever reason.		无论原因是什么，都与买卖双方的需求变化有关。
A price is simply the last trade. When more people want to buy than sell, buyers pay up and the price rises; news, results and rumours shift that balance.		价格就是最近一笔成交价。购买需求强于出售意愿时，买方愿意提高报价，价格上涨。新闻、业绩和传闻都会改变这种平衡。
Buyer		买方
I'll pay $10.05		我愿出 $10.05
last trade $10.05		最近成交价 $10.05
A big move on no news, in a small company, is often just a few traders - not a sign of anything.		小公司没有新闻却大幅波动，往往只是少数交易者造成，不一定代表特殊信号。
What actually moves a share price during the day?		交易日内，什么实际推动股价？
The balance of people wanting to buy and to sell		想买入与想卖出的人之间的供需平衡
The company's office opening hours		公司办公时间
The weather at the exchange		交易所的天气
The price your broker chooses		券商自行选择的价格
When the whole economy shrinks for a while - jobs, spending and profits together.		整体经济在一段时间内萎缩，就业、消费和利润一起走弱。
A recession is a broad fall in economic activity lasting months. A common rule of thumb is two quarters in a row of shrinking GDP (everything a country produces). Jobs get scarcer, people spend less, profits fall.		经济衰退是持续数月的广泛经济活动下降。常见经验指标是 GDP 连续两个季度下降，GDP 反映一国的总产出。就业减少、消费下降、利润走低。
Shares often fall before a recession is announced and recover before it ends - the news and the prices don't move on the same clock.		股价往往在衰退公布前下跌，也可能在衰退结束前恢复，新闻和价格并不同步。
A common rule of thumb for a recession is...		判断经济衰退的常见经验指标是……
Two quarters in a row of shrinking GDP		GDP 连续两个季度下降
Any day the stock market falls		股市任何一天收跌
Inflation above 2%		通胀超过 2%
Interest rates going up once		利率上调一次
"Guaranteed", "urgent", "secret" and "send crypto" - walk away.		“保证收益”“紧急”“秘密”“发送加密货币”，都是应当警惕的信号。
Real investments carry risk and never promise a fixed big return. Scams lean on urgency, secrecy, fake screenshots, celebrity videos and pressure to pay in crypto or gift cards.		真实投资有风险，不会保证固定高收益。骗局常利用紧迫感、保密要求、假截图、名人视频，并催促用加密货币或礼品卡付款。
Stranger		陌生人
guaranteed 30% a month!		保证每月 30% 收益！
(sends crypto)		（发送加密货币）
(blocks you)		（将你拉黑）
Pump-and-dump groups hype a tiny stock so the organisers can sell to the people they excited. Recovery services that promise to get scam money back for a fee are scams too.		拉高出货群会炒作小盘股，让组织者卖给被煽动的买家。承诺收费追回被骗资金的“追回服务”也可能是骗局。
A stranger promises guaranteed returns if you send crypto to their wallet. What is it?		陌生人要求你向其钱包发送加密货币，并保证收益，这是什么？
A scam		骗局
A normal crypto deal		正常的加密货币交易
A bank product		银行产品
A government programme		政府项目
A slice of a company, a scoreboard, and a basket.		公司的小份额、市场记分牌，以及一篮子资产。
A share is a small piece of one company. An index tracks a group of companies to show how a market is doing. An ETF is a fund you can buy that holds a whole basket, often following an index.		股票是一家公司的少量所有权。指数跟踪一组公司，反映市场表现。ETF 是可交易基金，持有一篮子资产，通常跟踪某个指数。
buy 1 share of a company		买入某公司 1 股
you own a slice		你拥有其中一小份
One company can fall a lot on its own news; a basket spreads that out - which is why indexes move less wildly than single stocks.		一家公司的消息可能导致股价大跌，而组合会分散这种影响，所以指数通常比单只股票波动小。
What is an index like the S&P 500 or the TSX?		S&P 500 或 TSX 这样的指数是什么？
A scoreboard that tracks a group of companies		跟踪一组公司的市场表现指标
A single company's share price		一家公司的股价
A bank account with interest		有利息的银行账户
A government bond		政府债券
Under the mattress, in savings, or invested - three very different paths.		床垫下的现金、储蓄与投资，是三条不同路径。
Cash in a drawer earns nothing and loses to inflation. A savings account pays a little and is safe. A broad index fund has historically grown far more over long periods - with big drops on the way, and nothing is ever guaranteed.		抽屉里的现金没有收益，还会受通胀侵蚀。储蓄账户有少量利息、较稳健。广泛分散的指数基金，历史上长期增长通常更高，但途中有大幅下跌，未来从无保证。
The higher the possible growth, the bigger the swings. Money for next year's rent wants safety; money for 20 years from now can ride the swings.		潜在增长越高，波动通常越大。明年房租更需要安全性，20 年后才用的资金则有更长时间承受波动。
Why does money for next year's rent usually not go into shares?		为什么明年的房租通常不放进股票？
A fall could hit just when you need it		恰好需要钱时，市场可能下跌
Shares are illegal for rent		用股票支付房租违法
Banks forbid it		银行禁止这样做
Shares only pay out after 30 years		股票要 30 年后才有回报
Revocation - cancelling a passport early		撤销：提前作废护照
A list of certificates that must not be trusted any more.		不应再被信任的证书清单。
When a private key leaks, the issuer withdraws the certificate before its end date and lists its serial number (CRL) - or answers "revoked" when asked (OCSP).		私钥泄漏时，签发机构会在到期前撤销证书，将序列号列入 CRL，或在 OCSP 查询中返回“已撤销”。
is serial 3003 still good?		序列号 3003 仍有效吗？
good (signed answer)		有效（带签名的应答）
trust it for now		目前可以信任
Checking means asking the issuer over the internet, so it can be slow or blocked - AdeeboMeter only asks if you turn it on in Settings.		检查需要通过互联网询问签发机构，因此可能慢或被阻止。AdeeboMeter 仅在设置中开启后查询。
What does a revocation list (CRL) hold?		证书撤销列表 CRL 保存什么？
Serial numbers of certificates withdrawn early		提前撤销的证书序列号
Every website's address		所有网站地址
Expired passwords		过期密码
The fastest servers		最快的服务器
Adaptive quality - why the picture goes blurry		自适应画质：画面为什么变模糊
Blurrier beats spinning.		模糊一些，总比转圈等待好。
Every chunk comes in several qualities. The player measures how fast the last chunks arrived and picks the next one to fit.		每个片段都有多档画质。播放器测量此前片段的下载速度，再选择适合的下一档。
Player		播放器
next chunk in 4K		下一片段使用 4K
(slow - took 6 s for 4 s of video)		（慢了，4 s 视频下载花了 6 s）
next chunk in HD instead		下一片段改用 HD
HD chunk (on time)		HD 片段（按时到达）
A line that keeps dipping makes the picture switch between sharp and blurry - a cable or a closer router usually steadies it.		线路反复降速会让画面在清晰和模糊之间切换，使用网线或靠近路由器通常能改善。
The speed drops for a minute. What does a modern player usually do?		速度下降一分钟，现代播放器通常怎么做？
Picks a lower quality for the next chunks so it keeps playing		后续片段选择较低画质，以保持播放
Stops and waits for the speed to return		停止并等待速度恢复
Switches to 4K to catch up		切换到 4K 追赶进度
Closes the app		关闭应用
Where the video comes from - CDNs		视频从哪里来：CDN
A copy of the show in a building near you.		附近机房中的节目副本。
Big services copy popular videos to servers inside or near your provider's network (a content delivery network), so a stream rarely crosses the whole internet.		大型平台将热门视频复制到运营商网络内部或附近的内容分发网络 CDN，因此播放通常无需穿越整个互联网。
Service		服务
play this show		播放这个节目
get it from the server in your city		从你所在城市的服务器获取
When the nearby copy is busy or down, the player is sent further away - the stream server's delay jumps while your own line is fine.		附近副本繁忙或离线时，播放器会转向更远的服务器，此时播放服务器延迟升高，但你自己的线路仍正常。
Why do Netflix, YouTube and others keep copies of videos close to viewers?		Netflix、YouTube 等为什么在观众附近保存视频副本？
Shorter trips mean faster, steadier streams		传输距离更短，播放更快、更稳定
Copies are cheaper to film		副本的拍摄成本更低
It hides the video from the internet		能对互联网隐藏视频
Routers can only reach nearby servers		路由器只能到达附近服务器
The first broken hop is the culprit.		第一个出问题的环节就是原因所在。
AdeeboMeter checks each hop of the path - PC, Wi-Fi, router, provider, the stream's server. Everything after the first broken hop is a victim, not a cause.		AdeeboMeter 逐段检查电脑、Wi-Fi、路由器、运营商和播放服务器。第一个故障环节之后的异常只是受影响，并非原因。
2 ms - fine		2 ms，正常
Evening-only dips with a healthy router usually mean the provider's busy hours - the monthly report gives you the evidence to show them.		路由器正常，只有晚间降速，通常提示运营商高峰拥堵。月度报告可提供联系运营商的证据。
Your router answers fast, but the internet check loses replies. Whose fault?		路由器应答很快，但外网检查丢包，问题在哪里？
Your provider's - the trouble is past your router		运营商一侧，问题在路由器之外
Your Wi-Fi's		你的 Wi-Fi
The PC's		电脑
The video's		视频
Small chunks, downloaded a little ahead of what you watch.		小片段下载，比当前播放略微提前。
The player downloads the video in chunks of a few seconds and keeps some ready before you need them - that store is the buffer.		播放器将视频按几秒一段下载，提前备好后续内容，这些预存内容就是缓冲。
chunk 1 please (4 s of video)		请给我片段 1（4 s 视频）
chunk 1		片段 1
chunk 2 please		请给我片段 2
chunk 2 (buffer: 8 s ready)		片段 2（缓冲中已备好 8 s）
When chunks arrive slower than you watch them, the buffer empties and the spinner appears.		片段下载速度跟不上播放时，缓冲耗尽，就会出现转圈等待。
What is a video's "buffer"?		视频“缓冲”是什么？
Video already downloaded, waiting to be shown		已下载、等待播放的视频
A setting that makes video sharper		让视频更清晰的设置
The server's copy of the film		服务器上的影片副本
A cable that carries the picture		传输画面的网线
Live TV over the internet - less buffer, less forgiveness.		网络直播：缓冲更少，更怕波动。
A live channel can't download far ahead - the next minute hasn't happened yet - so it keeps only a few seconds in the buffer.		直播无法提前下载很远，因为下一分钟还没发生，所以只能保留几秒缓冲。
Channel server		频道服务器
next few seconds, please		请给我接下来几秒
2 s of live video		2 s 直播视频
next 2 s		接下来的 2 s
(late - the picture freezes)		（迟到了，画面冻结）
Many stutters on a live channel come from the channel's own server; when your router and provider look fine, the record says "the stream's server".		直播卡顿也可能来自频道自己的服务器。路由器和运营商检查正常时，记录会注明“播放服务器”。
Why does live TV stutter more easily than a movie?		直播为什么比电影更容易卡顿？
It keeps only a few seconds of buffer, so short dips show		只有几秒缓冲，短暂降速就会影响播放
Live TV is a much bigger file		直播文件大得多
Live TV uses a slower kind of cable		直播使用更慢的网线
Movies are stored on your router		电影保存在路由器上

Learn why		了解原因
The chain of trust - a ladder		信任链：一架梯子
Root at the top, your certificate at the bottom.		顶端是根证书，底端是你的网站证书。
Your PC trusts a few roots. A root signs a middle (intermediate) certificate, which signs the site's - each link checked up to a root.		电脑信任若干根证书。根证书签发中间证书，中间证书再签发网站证书，逐级验证直到可信根。
I vouch for you (signed)		我为你担保（已签名）
The classic break: the server forgets the middle certificate. Browsers often find it anyway; apps and devices don't. Toolbox › Chain check shows the ladder.		常见故障是服务器漏发中间证书。浏览器往往还能找到，应用和设备则未必。“工具箱 › 证书链检查”可显示信任梯子。
A site works in a browser but an app says "untrusted". What's the usual cause?		网站在浏览器中正常，应用却提示“不受信任”，常见原因是什么？
The server doesn't send its middle certificate		服务器没有发送中间证书
The site is down		网站离线
The DNS is wrong		DNS 错误
The Wi-Fi is weak		Wi-Fi 信号弱
About 25 Mb/s - steady, not just at the speed test.		约 25 Mb/s，而且要持续稳定，不是只在测速时达到。
SD needs about 3 Mb/s, HD about 5, Full HD about 8 and 4K about 25 - and the line has to hold it the whole time, not just for a moment.		SD 约需 3 Mb/s，HD 约 5，Full HD 约 8，4K 约 25，而且线路需始终保持，而非仅短暂达到。

. Every release is built by GitHub from the public source, with its SHA256 fingerprint and build provenance (		。每个版本均由 GitHub 从公开源码构建，并提供 SHA256 指纹和构建来源证明（
). To remove it: Settings › Apps › AdeeboMeter › Uninstall.		）。卸载路径：设置 › 应用 › AdeeboMeter › 卸载。
From 1.1.5, releases are code-signed by		自 1.1.5 起，版本由以下开发者签名：
with a Certum open-source certificate, timestamped so signatures outlive it. Every release file is built by this project's GitHub Actions workflow from the public source — never on a personal PC — with every action pinned to an exact commit; the author then signs that exe and nothing else, and the release lists the fingerprint of the exe exactly as GitHub built it (covered by build provenance) beside the signed one. Each release is approved by hand. Team roles (committer, reviewer, approver):		，使用 Certum 开源证书，并带时间戳，让签名在证书到期后仍可验证。每个发布文件都由本项目的 GitHub Actions 从公开源码构建，绝不在个人电脑上构建；各 action 均固定到确切提交。作者仅签署该 exe，发布页同时列出 GitHub 原始构建和签名文件的指纹，并提供构建来源证明。每个版本均手动批准。团队角色包括提交、审核和批准：
, with multi-factor authentication.		，并启用多因素认证。
AdeeboMeter will not transfer any information to other networked systems unless you ask it to. It never listens, has no telemetry, no account and no updater. Checks and tools contact only what you typed or what they name on screen;		除非你要求，AdeeboMeter 不会向其他网络系统传输信息。不监听入站，无遥测、账号或自动更新器。检查和工具仅连接你输入的目标，或屏幕上明确注明的目标；
lists every packet your settings send.		列出当前设置会发送的每种数据包。
Off by default. Switched on for 5 minutes to 3 hours, they send public IP addresses only — never private addresses or names you typed — to		默认关闭。开启时可设为 5 分钟至 3 小时，仅发送公网 IP，绝不发送私网地址或你输入的名称，接收服务为
, then turn themselves off. Everything else stays on your PC; uninstalling removes it.		，之后自动关闭。其他内容保留在电脑上，卸载时移除。

A small, quiet Windows companion. One glance tells you whether your PC, your Wi-Fi, your provider, DNS or the internet is the problem — and what to do about it.		一个小而安静的 Windows 伴侣。一看就知道是你的 PC、你的 Wi-Fi、你的运营商、DNS 还是互联网出现了问题，以及如何解决。

Is something eating my CPU?		有什么东西在吃我的CPU吗？
Some hops time out. Is that bad?		一些跃点超时。那很糟糕吗？
"Must be the Wi-Fi." Restart the router. Hope.		“一定是Wi-Fi。”重新启动路由器。希望。
"Your Wi-Fi signal dropped to 38% when the calls started stuttering. Move closer to the access point."		“当通话开始卡顿时，你的 Wi-Fi 信号下降到 38%。靠近接入点。”
AdeeboMeter does all of that for you, quietly, every second — and puts the fix right after the finding.		AdeeboMeter 每秒都会悄悄地为你完成所有这些工作，并在发现问题后立即进行修复。
A few dozen tiny jobs a minute. Anything heavy runs only while you look at it, and stops by itself.		每分钟几十个小工作。任何重物只有在你注视它时才会运行，并自行停止。
No server, account or update check. It checks at every start that it opens no listening port.		没有服务器、账号或更新检查。它在每次启动时都会检查是否未打开侦听端口。
Only what Windows shows a normal user. If Windows says no, that part simply doesn't show.		仅Windows显示的是普通用户。如果 Windows 说“否”，则该部分根本不会显示。
Plain C#, built by the compiler already inside Windows. Nothing to install, nothing hidden.		纯 C#，由 Windows 内部的编译器构建。无需安装，无需隐藏。
Every message has a friendly and a plain version. The fix always comes right after the joke.		每条消息都有一个友好且简单的版本。笑话过后总会有解决办法。
The screen only ever reads finished snapshots — painting can never slow the Monitor down.		屏幕只读取完成的快照——绘画永远不会减慢显示器的速度。
One copy only. Loads settings. Refuses to run if it is listening on any port.		仅一份。加载设置。如果正在侦听任何端口，则拒绝运行。
The Monitor's two threads start. Tray icon and corner widget appear.		监视器的两个线程启动。出现托盘图标和角小部件。

Real lookups, cache skipped. A second opinion from 1.1.1.1 catches filters.		真正的查找，跳过缓存。 1.1.1.1 的第二个意见捕获了过滤器。
Which adapter Windows would use — found without sending anything.		Windows 将使用哪个适配器 - 无需发送任何内容即可找到。
Four echoes: delay and loss.		四种回声：延迟和丢失。
The three-way handshake to your port, timed.		与你的端口进行三向握手，定时。
1.2 / 1.3 only, Windows validates. Untrusted? Nothing is sent.		仅 1.2 / 1.3，Windows 有效。不受信任？没有发送任何内容。
One HEAD request: status line and time to first byte.		一个 HEAD 请求：状态行和第一个字节的时间。
"Don't fragment" pings find the biggest packet that fits.		“不分段”ping 会找到适合的最大数据包。
MTR-style trace: 3 rounds per hop, all hops at once.		MTR 式跟踪：每跳 3 轮，所有跳一次。
Double NAT and carrier-grade NAT (100.64.x.x) spotted from the hop addresses.		从跃点地址发现双 NAT 和运营商级 NAT (100.64.x.x)。
From the packet size: 1492 = PPPoE; smaller = VPN, LTE or satellite.		从数据包大小来看：1492 = PPPoE；较小 = VPN、LTE 或卫星。
A refusal faster than the server is far = something in between said no. Ping works, port silent = dropped.		拒绝比服务器快得多=介于两者之间的东西说不。 Ping 有效，端口静默 = 已丢弃。
Load-balanced hops, reroutes, where the delay jumps, hidden tunnel hops, the return path — and, with online lookups, the network that owns each hop.		负载平衡的跳跃、重新路由、延迟跳跃、隐藏隧道跳跃、返回路径，以及通过在线查找拥有每个跳跃的网络。
Windows tools by full System32 path, fixed arguments, a timeout, inside a job object that dies with the app.		Windows 工具由完整的 System32 路径、固定参数、超时、随应用程序终止的作业对象内部组成。
Destinations checked strictly before anything runs. Capture paths fenced inside AdeeboMeter's own folder.		目的地在运行之前经过严格检查。捕获 AdeeboMeter 自己的文件夹内的路径。
Only TLS 1.2 / 1.3; Windows validates certificates; nothing is sent over an untrusted connection.		仅TLS 1.2 / 1.3； Windows 验证证书；不通过不受信任的连接发送任何内容。
For the whole process — a DLL planted next to the exe in Downloads is never loaded.		在整个过程中，“下载”中 exe 旁边植入的 DLL 永远不会被加载。
No updater, no binary serializers, no XML parser, no secrets in the code. RADIUS secrets and SNMP communities / v3 passwords are masked and never kept.		没有更新程序，没有二进制序列化程序，没有 XML 解析器，代码中没有秘密。 RADIUS 秘密和 SNMP 社区/v3 密码被屏蔽并且永远不会保留。
Built by GitHub from this source, actions pinned to exact commits. The build can only read; a separate release step signs provenance. SHA-256 on every file, 0 analyzer findings.		由 GitHub 从此源构建，操作固定到确切的提交。构建只能读取；单独的发布步骤标志着出处。 SHA-256 在每个文件上，0 分析器结果。
of one CPU core with only the widget		一个 CPU 核心，仅包含小部件
with the engineer view open — zero the moment you leave		工程师视角打开——离开时归零
memory budget, shown live in the footer		内存预算，在页脚中实时显示
checks a minute: Penny · Balanced · Gamer (with its game-server pings)		检查一下：Penny · Balanced · Gamer（及其游戏服务器 ping）
Fixed-size rings; capped lists (300 events, 3,000 conversations, 7 days).		固定尺寸的戒指；上限列表（300 事件、3,000 对话、7 天）。
The widget checks the mouse 20×/s near the corner, 4×/s otherwise, 2×/s when hidden.		该小部件在角附近检查鼠标 20×/s，否则检查 4×/s，隐藏时检查 2×/s。
Windows, timers, bitmaps and helper tools released on close — and the self-test proves it.		Windows、定时器、位图和辅助工具已于近期发布——自测试证明了这一点。
Need an internet service — only through the opt-in, timed online lookups. Otherwise router names tell the story.		需要互联网服务——只能通过选择加入、定时在线查找。否则，路由器名称就能说明问题。
Needs raw sockets (admin) — traces use ICMP.		需要原始套接字（管理员）- 跟踪使用 ICMP。
Needs admin to switch on — per-app traffic comes from live capture instead.		需要管理员打开 - 每个应用程序的流量来自实时捕获。
Admin-only on some Windows builds — then names come from live capture.		某些 Windows 版本上仅限管理员 - 然后名称来自实时捕获。
Only to a server you run (iperf3, bufferbloat, TWAMP-light) — AdeeboMeter never listens.		仅针对你运行的服务器（iperf3、bufferbloat、TWAMP-light）——AdeeboMeter 从不监听。
Can only be inferred, never seen.		只能推测，没见过。
Windows usually clears a marking a program asks for unless a QoS policy allows it — only a capture proves it.		Windows 通常会清除程序请求的标记，除非 QoS 策略允许——只有捕获才能证明这一点。
Moving between a 100% and a 200% screen: Windows scales the window, slightly soft.		在 100% 和 200% 屏幕之间移动：Windows 缩放窗口，稍微柔和。

It's already running, bottom-right. Tray icon or Ctrl+Alt+A for the full story.		它已经在运行了，右下角。托盘图标或 Ctrl+Alt+A 查看完整故事。

Messages between Your PC and DNS, in order		你的 PC 和 DNS 之间的消息（按顺序）
Your PC to DNS:		你的电脑到 DNS：
DNS to Your PC:		DNS 到你的电脑：
Your PC → Server: SYN		你的电脑 → 服务器：SYN
Server → Your PC: SYN-ACK		服务器 → 你的 PC：SYN-ACK
Your PC → Server: ACK + TLS hello		你的电脑 → 服务器：ACK + TLS 你好
Server → Your PC: certificate + key		服务器→你的PC：证书+密钥
Your PC → Server: GET /		你的电脑 → 服务器：GET /
Server → Your PC: 200 OK + the page		服务器→你的电脑：200 OK +页面

Teams to Teams service:		Teams 至 Teams 服务：
Teams service to Teams:		Teams 服务至 Teams：

Your PC to Router:		你的电脑到路由器：

Internet → Your PC: reply after 21 ms, jitter 2 ms, 0% lost		互联网→你的电脑：21 ms后回复，抖动2 ms，0%丢失

AdeeboMeter for gaming - whose lag is it?		AdeeboMeter 游戏 - 谁的滞后？
Whose lag is it?		到底哪里导致卡顿？
Valorant, Fortnite, League of Legends, Counter-Strike 2, Roblox and more - AdeeboMeter knows their servers. It watches the ones you play on, notices when a game opens, and tells you whether the lag was you, your Wi-Fi, your provider or the game.		《Valorant》、《堡垒之夜》、《英雄联盟》、《反恐精英》2、Roblox 等 - AdeeboMeter 了解他们的服务器。它会监视你玩的游戏，注意到游戏何时打开，并告诉你延迟是由你、你的 Wi-Fi、你的运营商还是游戏造成的。

, which records 10 seconds of that protocol on your own PC (needs Wireshark).		，它在你自己的 PC 上记录该协议的 10 秒（需要 Wireshark）。

in AdeeboMeter, right under your card. Or fill in the four lines here - no account needed. The check runs straight away; if the code matches, you're on this page within a couple of minutes.		在 AdeeboMeter 中，就在你的卡下方。或者在此处填写四行 - 无需账号。检查会立即运行；如果代码匹配，你将在几分钟内进入此页面。
